Let's formalize exact change in convolution for transfer δ from src s to dst d (database). Let a0 array, b0. New a=s; for each k:
q_k = ∑_{i+j=k} a_i a_j (ordered all pairs within bounds).
Let Δ_s=-δ, Δ_d=δ.
Change = -δ ∂q/∂a_s + δ ∂q/∂a_d + second order. Derivative at a (ordered) =2a_{k-s} if 0≤k-s<n (with a0 original) plus? For second, cross terms: if modifications same index? For general, q is not strictly quadratic due to variables appearing twice; exact quadratic expansion: q_new = q + δ (2a_{k-d} - 2a_{k-s}) + δ² * C_k, where C_k = coefficient of δ².
C_k =? We can compute by considering all pair types:
- term i=s,j=s: if 2s=k: (-δ)^2 -> +δ².
- term i=d,j=d: if 2d=k: +δ².
- term i=s,j=d: if s+d=k: change 2(a_s-δ)(a_d+δ) -2a_s a_d = 2δ(a_s-a_d) -2δ². The linear part already captured? Derivative linear for d at -s: 2(a_{k-d}-a_{k-s}) =2(a_s - a_d). matches. Second -2δ².
- term i=d,j=s: included ordered separately same; if s≠d, there are two cross terms each? In convolution ordered, q includes a_s a_d and a_d a_s both if both indices valid. The total cross contribution before 2 a_s a_d, after 2(a_s-δ)(a_d+δ), change linear 2δ(a_s-a_d) -2δ². So C=-2 for s+d=k (s≠d). If s=d, not.
Additionally, if k-d = s (same as s+d=k) derivative linear for d term includes a_s; if k-s=d includes a_d. Our derivative formula handles both; no double beyond.
What about if k-s = s (2s=k) and k-d=s? Could overlap? C for diagonal +δ². If both 2s=k and s+d=k implies d=s, not.
Also if k-d=d and s+d=k. So C_k:
C=0 initially.
if 2*s == k: C += 1
if 2*d == k: C += 1
if s + d == k: C += -2 (if s!=d; if s=d then this would conflict: q includes a_s^2 not 2 a_s^2; our treating cross double wrong. So only if s!=d.)
But is linear derivative exact with original a? For diagonal s, linear -2a_s δ; new (a_s-δ)^2 = a_s² -2a_sδ + δ²; ok. For diagonal d, +2a_dδ+δ². For cross, linear 2(a_s? let's check derivative formula 2a_{k-d}-2a_{k-s}. If s+d=k, a_{k-d}=a_s, a_{k-s}=a_d, linear 2(a_s-a_d), matches. Good. If also s+d=k and 2s=k impossible unless d=s. So exact.
Thus for each active k, q_new(k)= q + δ L_k + δ² C_k with L_k=2*(val_d - val_s) where val_d = a_{k-d} if index in [0,n-1] else 0; val_s similarly. C as above.

Pseudo:
```
def active_cd(a0, time_limit, seed=0, initial_K=60):
    n = len(a0)
    a = normalize(a0)

```
def vals_for_offsets(a, k_idx, offsets):
    out = np.empty((len(offsets), len(k_idx)))
    for r,off in enumerate(offsets):
        idx = k_idx - off
        mask = (idx>=0)&(idx<n)
        v = np.zeros(len(k_idx))
        v[mask] = a[idx[mask]]
        out[r]=v
    return out
```

Detailed implementation of `active_cd`:
```
def active_cd(a0, time_limit, seed=0, verbose=False):
    n = len(a0)
    a = normalize(a0)
    conv = fast_conv(a)
    M = float(conv.max())
    best_a = a.copy(); best_m = M
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    K = max(20, min(120, int(0.04*n)))? For n=5000, 120? Use 60 start.
    max_pairs_per_iter = 800
    full_trials = 8
    stall = 0
    best_internal_score = 2*n*M? Not necessary.
    while time mon < limit:
       if M < best_m - 1e-15: best...
       remaining = limit - elapsed; if remaining <2 break
       # choose K adapt
       if stall > 500: K = min(2*n-1, K*2)
       if stall > 1000: # random perturb if improves
       ...
       K = min(K, 2*n-1)
       if K < 20: K=20
       # get top K indices
       if K >= 2*n-1:
          k_idx = np.arange(2*n-1)
          vals = conv
       else:
          k_idx = np.argpartition(conv, -K)[-K:]
          vals = conv[k_idx]
       # maybe sort k_idx? not needed.
       # p weights
       vmax=vals.max(); vmin=vals.min(); range = vmax-vmin
       p_top = ...
       p_full = np.zeros(2*n-1); p_full[k_idx]=p_top
       # V
       V = hankel_corr(p_full, a)
       Vmin = V.min(); Vmax=V.max()
       src_score = a * (V - Vmin)
       src_score = np.maximum(src_score, 0)
       # candidate lists
       src_count = min(n, max(30, min(150, int(0.015*n))))? For n5000 75; n9000 135.
       dst_count = min(n, max(15, min(80, int(0.01*n))))? For n5000 50; n9000 80.
       if src_count <1: src_count=1
       # Use argpartition
       src_idx = np.argpartition(src_score, -src_count)[-src_count:]
       dst_idx = np.argpartition(V, dst_count)[:dst_count]
       # also include random
       if n > 1:
          rand_s = rng.integers(0,n,size=min(20,n)); rand_d=...
          src_idx = np.unique(np.concatenate([src_idx, rand_s])) limited; dst_idx unique concat rand_d
       # Sort
       src_idx = src_idx[np.argsort(src_score[src_idx])[::-1]][:max(60, ...)]
       dst_idx = dst_idx[np.argsort(V[dst_idx])]
       q_top = conv[k_idx].copy()
       p_zeros = (p_top <= 1e-14) maybe use all top.
       # Precompute slices for src/dst if counts reasonable; if too many, limit.
       src_slice = src_idx[:max(50, src_count)]; dst_slice=dst_idx[:max(30,dst_count)]
       Sval = slice_vals(a, k_idx, src_slice)
       Dval = slice_vals(a, k_idx, dst_slice)
       candidates=[]
       tried=0
       for is, s in enumerate(src_slice):
          asrc=a[s]
          if asrc <= 1e-12: continue
          for id_, d in enumerate(dst_slice):
             if s==d: continue
             tried+=1
             L = 2.0*(Dval[id_,:] - Sval[is,:])
             C = (k_idx == 2*s) + (k_idx == 2*d) - 2.0*(k_idx == s+d)
             # weighted
             B = float(np.dot(p_top, L))
             # candidate deltas
             deltas=[]
             for frac in (0.08,0.18,0.32,0.55,0.8,0.95): deltas.append(frac*asrc)
             A = float(np.dot(p_top, C))
             if A > 1e-30:
                dpred = -B/(2*A)
                if 0.0 < dpred < asrc: deltas.append(dpred)
             elif B < 0:
                # concave/linear, maybe small or full
                deltas.append(0.02*asrc)
             # evaluate unique rounded
             best_pred = inf; best_delta=0
             # To speed, if weighted improvement positive skip
             # For each delta compute active max; vector over deltas maybe
             # We'll loop
             for delta in deltas:
                 if delta <= 1e-18 or delta > asrc - 1e-18: continue
                 # quick lower? compute max
                 # vals = q_top + delta*L + delta*delta*C
                 # Use float
                 maxv = np.max(q_top + delta*L + delta*delta*C)
                 if maxv < best_pred:
                    best_pred = maxv; best_delta=delta
             if best_pred < M - 1e-16 and best_delta > 0:
                 # Also maybe weighted improvement
                 candidates.append((best_pred, s, d, best_delta))
                 if len(candidates) >= max_pairs: break
          if len(candidates) >= max_pairs: break
       if candidates:
           candidates.sort(key=lambda x:x[0]) # lambda? Rule no lambda functions! Must avoid lambdas.
```
No lambdas! Need sort without lambda. Could use `candidates.sort()` tuple sorts by first. Candidate tuple (pred, s,d,delta) works. Or use `candidates.sort(key=...)` not lambda; can use no key if tuple. Good.

       for pred,s,d,delta in candidates[:full_trials]:

`slice_vals` function:
```
def slice_vals(a, k_idx, offsets):
    K=len(k_idx)
    out=np.empty((len(offsets),K), dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(a)
    for r,off in enumerate(offsets):
       off=int(off)
       idx=k_idx - off
       mask=(idx>=0)&(idx<n)
       out[r,:]=0.0
       out[r,mask]=a[idx[mask]]
    return out
```

def hankel_corr(p, a):

`fast_conv(a)` with full length 2n-1:
```
def fast_conv(a):
   n=len(a)
   if n==0: return np.array([])
   size=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
   F=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
   return np.fft.irfft(F*F, size)[:2*n-1]
```

def try_multiplicative(a, M, rng, max_m=20):
   n=len(a)
   i = np.arange(n)
   for attempt in range(200):
       m = rng.integers(1, max_m+1)
       phi = rng.uniform(0,2π)
       delta = rng.normal(0,0.2)
       factor = np.exp(delta*np.cos(2π*m*i/n+phi))
       trial=normalize(a*factor)
       Mnew=fast_conv(trial).max()
       if Mnew < M: return trial, Mnew
   return None
```

`fast_conv`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0]*a[0]], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:2*n-1]
```
If a contains inf? normalize avoids.

`hankel_corr`:
```
def hankel_corr(p, a):
    n=len(a)
    m=len(p)
    if n==1:
       return np.array([p[0]*a[0] if m>0 else 0.0])
    need = n + m -1
    size = 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fr = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fr, size)
    end = 2*n - 2
    start = n - 1
    # return c[end], c[end-1], ..., c[start]
    return c[end : start - 1 : -1]
```
Check length: c[end : start-1 : -1] with Python inclusive end: from end down to start. For n=2 end2 start1 -> c[2:0:-1] length2. For n=1 start0? handled.
Does linear conv length need >= n+m-1; c beyond need may have cyclic wrap if size too small? size next pow2 >= need, good.

But `p[:m]` reversed length m; zeros pad. Good.

Test formula with simple n=2, p=[p0,p1,p2], a=[a0,a1]. V0 = p0 a0 + p1 a1? Formula V_i =∑_k p_k a_{k-i}.
i=0: k=0 p0 a0; k=1 p1 a1; k=2 p2 a2 out0 => p0 a0 +p1 a1. Our c=conv(a, r) where r=[p2,p1,p0]. c_t:
t=0: a0 p2
t=1: a0 p1 + a1 p2
t=2: a0 p0 + a1 p1
t=3: a1 p0
end=2, d=0 -> c2 = a0 p0+a1 p1 correct. d=1 -> c1 = a0 p1+a1 p2. V1 =∑ p_k a_{k-1}: k=1 p1 a0; k=2 p2 a1 = a0 p1 + a1 p2 correct. Good.

            best_m=M; best_a=a.copy()
        # if no pairs K adaptive
        # choose active
```

```
def mirror_improve(a, M, max_steps=50):
   n=len(a)
   loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
   for step in range(max_steps):
       conv = fast_conv(a) # maybe use current M but top p needs conv; can pass conv? To save, compute if time.
       k_idx top K... maybe use 80
       p...
       V=hankel_corr(p,a)
       maxV=max(abs(V).max(),1e-18)
       eta = 0.2 / maxV
       best=None
       for mult in [0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
          trial = a * np.exp(-mult*eta*V)
          trial = normalize_seq(trial)
          tm=fast_conv(trial).max()
          if tm < M - 1e-14:
             # choose best among mult that improve
             if best is None or tm<best_m: best=trial,tb,tm
       if best: return best
       # no step reduces, break? maybe try smaller
       eta *=0.5
       if eta < 1e-6 / maxV? break
   return None
```

Maybe instead of mirror inside, do a final `smoothed_opt` using convex? Let's implement a full `adaptive_mirror(a, time_limit)` that uses topK p and mirror steps, with adaptive step, and can improve. It can run for e.g., 100s.

```
def mirror_cd(a0, time_limit, seed=0):
   a=normalize; conv=fast_conv; M=best_m
   t0; rng
   K=min(120, max(40,int(0.01*n)))
   step_count=0
   while time<limit:
      # p, V
      # choose η target based on t?
      maxV = max(max(V.abs),1e-18)
      base_eta = 0.1 / maxV
      accepted=False
      for mult in [0.5,1,2,4]:
          eta=base_eta*mult
          trial = a*np.exp(-eta*V)
          trial=normalize; tm=conv(trial).max()
          if tm < M - 1e-14:
             a=trial; conv=tb; M=tm; accepted=True; step_count++; break
      if not accepted:
          # try half previous? or adjust K
          if step_count % 10==0: K=min(300,K+10)
          # if no step for many, break or multiplicative
      update best
   return a
```
This could substitute previous AMD. It may find improvements if active CD misses. But mirror step direction V (weighted gradient) is same as active CD but global. Previous AMD with fixed step maybe slow; adaptive with line search by trying multiples should be better. Use 120s.

`scan_harmonic` implementation:
```
def scan_harmonic(n, time_limit, seed=0):
    t0=...
    if n < 50: ...
    local_a=None; local_int=inf; best_params=None
    i=np.arange(n)
    max_m=5
    phis=np.linspace(0, 2*np.pi, 18, endpoint=False)
    eps_list=(0.05,0.15,0.25,0.35,0.45,0.55,0.65,0.75,0.85,0.95)
    for m in range(1, max_m+1):
      for phi in phis:
        for eps in eps_list:
           if time>... break
           v=1+eps*cos
           if v.sum()<1e-12 continue
           a=v/v.sum()
           b=fast_conv(a); sc=2*n*b.max()
           if sc < local_int:
              local_int=sc; local_a=a.copy(); best_params=(m,phi,eps)
    # refine best params coords
    if local_a is not None and time left:
       m,phi,eps=best_params
       for it in range(100):
          improved=False
          for pm,pp,pe in [m, phi±0.05,10 grids eps±0.02...]:
           eval; if better update improved
          if not improved or timeout: break
    return local_a
```

          trial=normalize

       else:

def simple_length_scan(base_a, n_min, n_max, step, time_limit):

```
_POOL = []
_POOL_COUNTER = 0
POOL_MAX = 8

def reset_pool():
    global _POOL, _POOL_COUNTER
    _POOL=[]; _POOL_COUNTER=0

def consider_pool(a, label):
    global _POOL, _POOL_COUNTER
    a=normalize_seq(a)
    if a is None: return
    sc=internal_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return
    _POOL_COUNTER += 1
    item=(float(sc), _POOL_COUNTER, a)
    # If pool not full or item better than worst, insert
    if len(_POOL) < POOL_MAX:
        _POOL.append(item)
        # sort small list
        _POOL.sort()
        if len(_POOL)>POOL_MAX: del _POOL[-1]
    else:
        if sc < _POOL[-1][0] - 1e-12:
            # remove worst, append, sort
            del _POOL[-1]
            _POOL.append(item)
            _POOL.sort()
    # print if new best internal
    if not _POOL or sc < _POOL[0][0] - 1e-12:
        print(f"[{label}] internal={sc:.6f} n={len(a)}")
```

Implementation of `scan_harmonic` with refine. Need avoid too many evals if time_limit small.
```
def scan_harmonic(n, time_limit, seed=0):
    if n < 100 or time_limit < 2: return None
    t0=time.monotonic()
    i=np.arange(n)
    local_a=None; local_int=inf; best_m=1; best_phi=0.0; best_eps=0.5; best_seen=False
    phis = np.linspace(0.0, 2*np.pi, 16, endpoint=False)
    eps_list = (0.05,0.15,0.25,0.35,0.45,0.55,0.65,0.75,0.85,0.95)
    max_m = 5
    for m in range(1, max_m+1):
       for phi in phis:
          for eps in eps_list:
             if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
             v = 1.0 + eps*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*i/n + phi)
             v = np.maximum(v,0.0)
             ssum = v.sum()
             if ssum <= 1e-12: continue
             a = v/ssum
             sc = internal_score(a)
             if sc < local_int - 1e-12:
                 local_int=sc; local_a=a.copy(); best_m=m; best_phi=float(phi); best_eps=float(eps)
    if local_a is None: return None
    # refine
    if time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       refinements = 0
       improved=True
       # coordinate grid around best
       while improved and time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit - 5:
          improved=False
          # m candidates: best_m-1,best,best_m+1
          for m2 in (max(1,best_m-1), best_m, max(1,best_m+1)):
          # phi candidates: best_phi + offsets
          for dphi in (-0.15,-0.07,-0.03,0,0.03,0.07,0.15):
              phi2=(best_phi+dphi) % (2π)
              for deps in (-0.04,-0.02,0,0.02,0.04):
                  eps2=min(1.0,max(0.0,best_eps+deps))
                  if ... eval
          if not improved after one full: break
    return local_a
```
This could be many evals: 3*7*5=105 per loop; several loops. Time limit maybe 30s; OK. Need avoid long if n large. Each eval FFT. 105*0.5ms=0.05s for 5000. Fine.

But if `local_int` is inf due to all sums? no.

```python
"""
Search strategy for minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

Main ingredients:
1. Fast FFT-based convolution scores for score-invariant normalized sequences.
2. A candidate pool by internal score; final exact evaluate_sequence() calls
   are applied only to the best few candidates.
3. Resampling / length scanning to exploit better discrete lengths.
4. Low-dimensional harmonic seeds.
5. A strong active-set coordinate descent with exact quadratic line search
   for pairwise mass transfers.
6. Multiplicative low-frequency perturbations and projected mirror-descent
   steps for leaving local traps.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

# Global candidate pool.
POOL_MAX = 10
_ACC_TOL = 1e-14
_POOL_TOL = 1e-12

_POOL = []
_POOL_COUNTER = 0


def reset_pool():
    global _POOL, _POOL_COUNTER
    _POOL = []
    _POOL_COUNTER = 0


def normalize_seq(a):
    """Return a non-negative normalized copy of a, or None if invalid."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        a = np.ones_like(a)
        s = float(a.sum())
    return a / s


def fast_conv(a):
    """Full linear convolution a*a using FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 1 << ((2 * n - 2).bit_length())
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:2 * n - 1]


def internal_score(a):
    """
    Scale-invariant fast score:
        2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2
    This matches evaluate_sequence() for sequences with values in [0, 1000]
    up to FFT roundoff.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    b = fast_conv(a)
    m = float(b.max())
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def consider_pool(a, label):
    """Keep a small pool of the best internally scored sequences."""
    global _POOL, _POOL_COUNTER
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    if a is None:
        return
    sc = internal_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return

    old_best = _POOL[0][0] if len(_POOL) > 0 else float("inf")
    is_new_best = sc < old_best - _POOL_TOL

    _POOL_COUNTER += 1
    item = (float(sc), _POOL_COUNTER, a)

    if len(_POOL) < POOL_MAX:
        _POOL.append(item)
        _POOL.sort()
        if len(_POOL) > POOL_MAX:
            del _POOL[-1]
    else:
        if sc < _POOL[-1][0] - _POOL_TOL:
            del _POOL[-1]
            _POOL.append(item)
            _POOL.sort()

    if is_new_best:
        print(f"  pool best [{label}]: internal={sc:.6f}, n={len(a)}")


def interpolate_seq(a, new_n):
    """Resample a sequence to a new length, preserving nonnegativity and sum."""
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    old_n = len(a)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return None
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    out = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return normalize_seq(out)


def make_harmonic(n, m, eps, phi):
    """Nonnegative one-harmonic normalized sequence."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = 1.0 + eps * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x / float(n) + phi)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        v = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(v.sum())
    return v / s


def hankel_corr(p, a):
    """
    V_i = sum_k p_k * a_{k-i}, with out-of-bounds a indices as 0.
    Used as the gradient of active-set weighted convolution objectives.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    m = p.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([p[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)

    need = n + m - 1
    size = 1 << ((need - 1).bit_length())
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fr = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fr, size)

    end = 2 * n - 2
    start = n - 1
    return c[end : start - 1 : -1]


def slice_vals_for_offsets(a, k_idx, offsets):
    """For many offsets d, return a_{k-d} with zero when invalid."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    k_idx = np.asarray(k_idx, dtype=np.int64).reshape(-1)
    offsets = np.asarray(offsets, dtype=np.int64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    K = k_idx.size
    out = np.empty((offsets.size, K), dtype=np.float64)
    if offsets.size == 0:
        return out
    for r, off in enumerate(offsets):
        off = int(off)
        idx = k_idx - off
        mask = (idx >= 0) & (idx < n)
        out[r, :] = 0.0
        if np.any(mask):
            out[r, mask] = a[idx[mask]]
    return out


def multiplicative_improve(a, M, rng, max_evals=60):
    """
    Try low-frequency multiplicative perturbations:
        a_new = normalize(a * exp(delta*cos(2*pi*m*t + phi)))
    Accept only if the fast convolution peak decreases.
    """
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = len(a)
    if n < 10:
        return None
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    max_m = min(48, max(2, n // 64))
    two_pi_over_n = 2.0 * np.pi / float(n)

    for _ in range(int(max_evals)):
        m = int(rng.integers(1, max_m + 1))
        phi = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        delta = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.22))
        if abs(delta) < 0.03:
            delta = 0.05 if delta >= 0.0 else -0.05

        factor = np.exp(delta * np.cos(two_pi_over_n * m * x + phi))
        trial = normalize_seq(a * factor)
        if trial is None:
            continue
        tb = fast_conv(trial)
        tm = float(tb.max())
        if tm < M - _ACC_TOL:
            return trial, tb, tm
    return None


def scan_harmonic(n, time_limit, seed=0):
    """Grid search and local refinement over one-harmonic nonnegative shapes."""
    n = int(n)
    if n < 100 or time_limit <= 1.0:
        return None
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    two_pi_over_n = 2.0 * np.pi / float(n)

    local_a = None
    local_int = float("inf")
    best_m = 1
    best_phi = 0.0
    best_eps = 0.5

    phis = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, 16, endpoint=False)
    eps_list = (0.05, 0.15, 0.25, 0.35, 0.45, 0.55, 0.65, 0.75, 0.85, 0.95)
    max_m = 5

    for m in range(1, max_m + 1):
        for phi in phis:
            for eps in eps_list:
                if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
                    break
                a = make_harmonic(n, m, float(eps), float(phi))
                if a is None:
                    continue
                sc = internal_score(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_int - 1e-14:
                    local_int = sc
                    local_a = a.copy()
                    best_m = m
                    best_phi = float(phi)
                    best_eps = float(eps)

    if local_a is None:
        return None

    # Local refinement around the best grid point.
    if time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit - 5.0:
        for _ in range(8):
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit - 3.0:
                break
            improved = False
            m_cands = [max(1, best_m - 1), best_m, best_m + 1, best_m + 2]
            phi_offsets = [-0.20, -0.10, -0.05, -0.02, 0.0, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20]
            eps_offsets = [-0.05, -0.02, 0.0, 0.02, 0.05]

            for m2 in m_cands:
                if m2 > 12:
                    continue
                for dphi in phi_offsets:
                    phi2 = float((best_phi + dphi) % (2.0 * np.pi))
                    for deps in eps_offsets:
                        eps2 = float(min(1.0, max(0.0, best_eps + deps)))
                        a2 = make_harmonic(n, m2, eps2, phi2)
                        if a2 is None:
                            continue
                        sc2 = internal_score(a2)
                        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < local_int - 1e-13:
                            local_int = sc2
                            local_a = a2.copy()
                            best_m = m2
                            best_phi = phi2
                            best_eps = eps2
                            improved = True
            if not improved:
                break

    return local_a


def simple_length_scan(base_a, n_min, n_max, step, time_limit):
    """Resample the current best to several nearby lengths."""
    base_a = normalize_seq(base_a)
    if base_a is None or time_limit <= 1.0:
        return
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    n_min = int(max(500, n_min))
    n_max = int(min(9500, n_max))
    if n_min > n_max:
        n_min, n_max = n_max, n_min
    step = int(max(50, step))

    best_n = None
    best_int = float("inf")

    n = n_min
    while n <= n_max:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
            break
        a = interpolate_seq(base_a, n)
        if a is not None:
            sc = internal_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < best_int:
                    best_int = sc
                    best_n = n
                consider_pool(a, f"len{n}")
        n += step

    if best_n is not None:
        step2 = max(10, step // 10)
        lo = max(500, best_n - int(step))
        hi = min(9500, best_n + int(step))
        n = lo
        while n <= hi:
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
                break
            a = interpolate_seq(base_a, n)
            if a is not None:
                consider_pool(a, f"lenref{n}")
            n += step2


def active_cd(a0, time_limit, seed=0, K_start=70):
    """
    Active-set coordinate descent with exact quadratic line search
    for pairwise mass moves.

    Moving delta from s to d changes each convolution coefficient exactly:
        q_k(delta) = q_k + delta*L_k + delta^2*C_k
    where L_k and C_k are computed from s, d, and current a.
    """
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = len(a)
    if n < 4:
        return a

    conv = fast_conv(a)
    M = float(conv.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = M

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    maxK = min(2 * n - 1, 500)
    base_K = min(maxK, max(30, int(0.012 * n)))
    K = min(maxK, max(base_K, int(K_start)))
    stall = 0

    # Fractions of source mass to try in the 1-D line search.
    base_fracs = np.array([0.04, 0.08, 0.14, 0.22, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.8, 0.93, 0.999],
                          dtype=np.float64)

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed >= time_limit - 0.5:
            break

        if M < best_m - _ACC_TOL:
            best_m = M
            best_a = a.copy()

        # Active lags.
        ncong = 2 * n - 1
        if K >= ncong:
            k_idx = np.arange(ncong, dtype=np.int64)
            vals = conv
        else:
            k_idx = np.argpartition(conv, -K)[-K:]
            vals = conv[k_idx]

        vmax = float(vals.max())
        vmin = float(vals.min())
        spread = vmax - vmin
        if spread < 1e-18:
            p_top = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
        else:
            temp = max(spread / 5.0, 1e-12)
            p = np.exp((vals - vmax) / temp)
            ps = float(p.sum())
            if ps <= 1e-18:
                p_top = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
            else:
                p_top = p / ps

        # Gradient-like array V_i = sum_k p_k a_{k-i}.
        p_full = np.zeros(ncong, dtype=np.float64)
        p_full[k_idx] = p_top
        V = hankel_corr(p_full, a)

        Vmin = float(V.min())
        Vmax = float(V.max())
        if Vmax - Vmin < 1e-15:
            trial = multiplicative_improve(a, M, rng, 40)
            if trial is not None:
                a, conv, M = trial
                stall = 0
                K = base_K
                continue
            if stall > 100:
                break
            stall += 1
            continue

        # Candidate sources/destinations.
        src_score = a * (V - Vmin)
        src_score = np.maximum(src_score, 0.0)

        src_count = min(n, max(30, int(0.008 * n) + K // 2))
        src_count = min(src_count, 120)
        dst_count = min(n, max(18, int(0.006 * n) + K // 4))
        dst_count = min(dst_count, 80)

        if src_count >= n:
            src_idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            src_idx = np.argpartition(src_score, -src_count)[-src_count:]
        src_score_arr = src_score[src_idx]
        src_cnt = int(np.argmax(src_score_arr)) + 1
        if src_cnt > 0 and src_cnt <= src_idx.size:
            src_idx = src_idx[np.argpartition(src_score_arr, -src_cnt)[-src_cnt:]]
        src_idx = src_idx[np.argsort(src_score[src_idx])[::-1]]

        if dst_count >= n:
            dst_idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            dst_idx = np.argpartition(V, dst_count)[:dst_count]
        dst_idx = dst_idx[np.argsort(V[dst_idx])]

        # Add a bit of randomness for robustness.
        nrand = min(12, n)
        if nrand > 0:
            rand_s = rng.integers(0, n, size=nrand)
            rand_d = rng.integers(0, n, size=nrand)
            src_idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((src_idx, rand_s)))
            dst_idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((dst_idx, rand_d)))
            src_idx = src_idx[:min(130, len(src_idx))]
            dst_idx = dst_idx[:min(90, len(dst_idx))]

        src_slice = src_idx[:min(90, len(src_idx))]
        dst_slice = dst_idx[:min(50, len(dst_idx))]
        if len(src_slice) == 0 or len(dst_slice) == 0:
            stall += 1
            continue

        q_top = conv[k_idx].copy()
        Sval = slice_vals_for_offsets(a, k_idx, src_slice)
        Dval = slice_vals_for_offsets(a, k_idx, dst_slice)

        candidates = []
        max_candidates = 900
        tried = 0

        for is_idx, s in enumerate(src_slice):
            s = int(s)
            asrc = float(a[s])
            if asrc <= 1e-14:
                continue
            two_s = 2 * s
            for id_idx, d in enumerate(dst_slice):
                d = int(d)
                if s == d:
                    continue
                tried += 1
                if tried > 3500:
                    break

                # L_k = 2 * (a_{k-d} - a_{k-s}).
                L = 2.0 * (Dval[id_idx, :] - Sval[is_idx, :])

                # Linear weighted change.
                B = float(np.dot(p_top, L))
                if B > 1e-16:
                    continue

                # C_k: +1 at 2s, +1 at 2d, -2 at s+d.
                two_d = 2 * d
                sum_sd = s + d
                C = (k_idx == two_s) + (k_idx == two_d) - 2.0 * (k_idx == sum_sd)

                # Candidate deltas.
                deltas = base_fracs * asrc
                A = float(np.dot(p_top, C))
                if A > 1e-30:
                    dpred = -B / (2.0 * A)
                    if dpred > 1e-16 and dpred < asrc:
                        deltas = np.concatenate((deltas, np.array([dpred], dtype=np.float64)))
                elif A < -1e-30:
                    deltas = np.concatenate((deltas, np.array([0.02 * asrc], dtype=np.float64)))

                deltas = deltas[(deltas > 1e-16) & (deltas < asrc)]
                if deltas.size == 0:
                    continue

                # Evaluate exact active-set maximum at candidate deltas.
                vals = q_top[None, :] + deltas[:, None] * L[None, :] + (deltas[:, None] ** 2) * C[None, :]
                maxvals = vals.max(axis=1)
                imin = int(np.argmin(maxvals))
                pred = float(maxvals[imin])

                if pred < M - _ACC_TOL:
                    candidates.append((pred, s, d, float(deltas[imin])))
                    if len(candidates) >= max_candidates:
                        break
            if len(candidates) >= max_candidates:
                break

        if tried == 0:
            stall += 1
        else:
            if candidates:
                candidates.sort()
                accepted = False
                trials = 12
                for item in candidates[:trials]:
                    if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit - 1.0:
                        break
                    pred, s, d, delta = item
                    trial = a.copy()
                    trial[s] -= delta
                    trial[d] += delta
                    if trial[s] < 0.0 or trial[d] < 0.0:
                        continue
                    trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
                    st = float(trial.sum())
                    if st <= 1e-15:
                        continue
                    trial /= st
                    tb = fast_conv(trial)
                    tm = float(tb.max())
                    if tm < M - _ACC_TOL:
                        a = trial
                        conv = tb
                        M = tm
                        accepted = True
                        break
                if accepted:
                    stall = 0
                else:
                    stall += 1
            else:
                stall += 1

        # Stall recovery.
        if stall > 0:
            if stall % 25 == 0:
                K = min(maxK, int(K * 1.25) + 8)
            if stall > 80 and stall % 40 == 0:
                trial = multiplicative_improve(a, M, rng, 50)
                if trial is not None:
                    a, conv, M = trial
                    stall = 0
                    K = base_K
            if stall > 4000:
                break

    # Return the best sequence found within this call.
    return best_a


def mirror_cd(a0, time_limit, seed=0):
    """
    Projected mirror-descent on the simplex for the active weighted max objective.
    Uses exact NTT/FFT convolution and accepts only improving steps.
    """
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = len(a)
    if n < 4:
        return a

    conv = fast_conv(a)
    M = float(conv.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = M

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    maxK = min(2 * n - 1, 400)
    base_K = min(maxK, max(40, int(0.011 * n)))
    K = base_K
    stall = 0

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed >= time_limit - 0.5:
            break

        if M < best_m - _ACC_TOL:
            best_m = M
            best_a = a.copy()

        ncong = 2 * n - 1
        if K >= ncong:
            k_idx = np.arange(ncong, dtype=np.int64)
            vals = conv
        else:
            k_idx = np.argpartition(conv, -K)[-K:]
            vals = conv[k_idx]

        vmax = float(vals.max())
        vmin = float(vals.min())
        spread = vmax - vmin
        if spread < 1e-18:
            p_top = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
        else:
            temp = max(spread / 5.0, 1e-12)
            p = np.exp((vals - vmax) / temp)
            ps = float(p.sum())
            if ps <= 1e-18:
                p_top = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
            else:
                p_top = p / ps

        p_full = np.zeros(ncong, dtype=np.float64)
        p_full[k_idx] = p_top
        V = hankel_corr(p_full, a)

        Vmax = float(np.max(np.abs(V)))
        if Vmax <= 1e-18:
            trial = multiplicative_improve(a, M, rng, 40)
            if trial is not None:
                a, conv, M = trial
                stall = 0
                continue
            if stall > 100:
                break
            stall += 1
            continue

        base_eta = 0.1 / Vmax
        accepted = False
        for mult in (0.125, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
            eta = base_eta * float(mult)
            trial = a * np.exp(-eta * V)
            trial = normalize_seq(trial)
            if trial is None:
                continue
            tb = fast_conv(trial)
            tm = float(tb.max())
            if tm < M - _ACC_TOL:
                a = trial
                conv = tb
                M = tm
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 30 == 0:
                K = min(maxK, int(K * 1.2) + 10)
            if stall > 80 and stall % 40 == 0:
                trial = multiplicative_improve(a, M, rng, 50)
                if trial is not None:
                    a, conv, M = trial
                    stall = 0
                    K = base_K
            if stall > 1000:
                break

    return best_a


def final_choose_from_pool(max_evals=8):
    """Evaluate the best few pool candidates with the official evaluator."""
    if len(_POOL) == 0:
        return None, float("inf")

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    limit = min(max_evals, len(_POOL))

    for idx in range(limit):
        try:
            seq = _POOL[idx][2]
            lst = []
            for v in seq:
                vv = float(v)
                if vv < 0.0:
                    vv = 0.0
                if vv > 1000.0:
                    vv = 1000.0
                lst.append(vv)
            ev = evaluate_sequence(lst)
            if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_score:
                best_score = float(ev)
                best_seq = seq.copy()
        except Exception:
            continue

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = _POOL[0][2].copy()
        best_score = float(_POOL[0][0])
    return best_seq, best_score


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """
    Search for a best nonnegative step-function height sequence.
    """
    global _POOL, _POOL_COUNTER
    reset_pool()

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(120.0, min(float(budget_s) - 60.0, 1740.0))

    # Try to start from the known previous construction.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)) and float(p.sum()) > 1e-10:
            parent = normalize_seq(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        consider_pool(parent, "parent")

    # Fallback if no usable parent exists.
    if len(_POOL) == 0:
        n_fb = 4700
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_fb)
        a_fb = np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2
        consider_pool(a_fb, "fallback_sine")

    if len(_POOL) == 0:
        n_fb = 4700
        a_fb = make_harmonic(n_fb, 1, 0.5, 0.0)
        consider_pool(a_fb, "fallback_harm")

    if len(_POOL) == 0:
        raise RuntimeError("No valid candidate sequence was generated.")

    # Initial seeds: low-dimensional harmonic and length resampling.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    rem = total_budget - elapsed
    if rem > 120.0:
        n_best = len(_POOL[0][2])

        t_scan0 = min(35.0, rem * 0.04)
        if t_scan0 > 5.0:
            a_scan = scan_harmonic(n_best, t_scan0, seed=1)
            if a_scan is not None:
                consider_pool(a_scan, "harm0")

        rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
        t_len = min(35.0, rem * 0.03)
        if t_len > 5.0:
            simple_length_scan(
                _POOL[0][2],
                max(1500, n_best - 1400),
                min(8500, n_best + 1800),
                250,
                t_len
            )

    # Main local optimization at the current best length.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    rem = total_budget - elapsed
    t_active1 = min(rem * 0.40, 750.0)
    if t_active1 > 30.0:
        a1 = active_cd(_POOL[0][2], t_active1, seed=101, K_start=70)
        if a1 is not None:
            consider_pool(a1, "active1")

    # Mirror descent polish.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    rem = total_budget - elapsed
    t_mir1 = min(rem * 0.12, 180.0)
    if t_mir1 > 15.0:
        a2 = mirror_cd(_POOL[0][2], t_mir1, seed=102)
        if a2 is not None:
            consider_pool(a2, "mirror1")

    # Upscale to a larger length.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    rem = total_budget - elapsed
    n_best = len(_POOL[0][2])
    if rem > 220.0 and n_best < 8200:
        n_up = min(8800, max(n_best + 800, int(round(1.35 * n_best))))
        if n_up > n_best:
            a_up = interpolate_seq(_POOL[0][2], n_up)
            if a_up is not None:
                consider_pool(a_up, f"up{n_up}")

            # Choose the best starting sequence at this length.
            start = a_up
            up_int = internal_score(a_up) if a_up is not None else float("inf")

            # Optional harmonic seed at the larger length.
            rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
            t_scan1 = min(25.0, rem * 0.04)
            if t_scan1 > 5.0:
                a_scan1 = scan_harmonic(n_up, t_scan1, seed=103)
                if a_scan1 is not None:
                    consider_pool(a_scan1, f"harm{n_up}")
                    sc_scan1 = internal_score(a_scan1)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_scan1) and sc_scan1 < up_int:
                        start = a_scan1
                        up_int = sc_scan1

            # Also use pool best if it is already at this length and better.
            if len(_POOL[0][2]) == n_up:
                sc_pool0 = float(_POOL[0][0])
                if np.isfinite(sc_pool0) and sc_pool0 < up_int:
                    start = _POOL[0][2]
                    up_int = sc_pool0

            rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
            t_active2 = min(rem * 0.45, 720.0)
            if t_active2 > 30.0 and start is not None:
                a3 = active_cd(start, t_active2, seed=104, K_start=max(70, int(0.012 * n_up)))
                if a3 is not None:
                    consider_pool(a3, f"active_up{n_up}")

    # Mirror descent polish.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    rem = total_budget - elapsed
    t_mir2 = min(rem * 0.20, 240.0)
    if t_mir2 > 15.0:
        a4 = mirror_cd(_POOL[0][2], t_mir2, seed=105)
        if a4 is not None:
            consider_pool(a4, "mirror2")

    # Second upscale if there is still time and the best length is not yet large.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    rem = total_budget - elapsed
    n_best = len(_POOL[0][2])
    if rem > 180.0 and n_best < 8800:
        n_up2 = min(9200, int(round(1.3 * n_best)))
        if n_up2 > n_best:
            a_up2 = interpolate_seq(_POOL[0][2], n_up2)
            if a_up2 is not None:
                consider_pool(a_up2, f"up{n_up2}")
                start2 = a_up2
                if len(_POOL[0][2]) == n_up2:
                    start2 = _POOL[0][2]

                # A short harmonic seed at this second larger length.
                rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
                t_scan2 = min(18.0, rem * 0.03)
                if t_scan2 > 5.0:
                    a_scan2 = scan_harmonic(n_up2, t_scan2, seed=106)
                    if a_scan2 is not None:
                        consider_pool(a_scan2, f"harm{n_up2}")
                        if internal_score(a_scan2) < internal_score(start2):
                            start2 = a_scan2

                rem = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
                t_active3 = min(rem * 0.55, 600.0)
                if t_active3 > 30.0:
                    a5 = active_cd(start2, t_active3, seed=107, K_start=max(80, int(0.012 * n_up2)))
                    if a5 is not None:
                        consider_pool(a5, f"active_up2_{n_up2}")

    # Final active-set polish.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    rem = total_budget - elapsed
    t_final = min(rem * 0.75, 300.0)
    if t_final > 15.0:
        a_final = active_cd(_POOL[0][2], t_final, seed=108, K_start=90)
        if a_final is not None:
            consider_pool(a_final, "final_active")

    # Choose and evaluate a few best internal candidates exactly.
    best_seq, best_score = final_choose_from_pool(max_evals=8)

    # If still no exact candidate could be evaluated, fall back to pool best.
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = _POOL[0][2].copy()
        best_score = float(_POOL[0][0])

    try:
        final_list = [float(v) for v in best_seq]
        final_score = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
    except Exception:
        final_list = [float(max(0.0, v)) for v in best_seq]
        final_score = best_score

    print(f"FINAL selected: score={float(final_score) if np.isfinite(final_score) else 'inf'},"
          f" n={len(final_list)}, internal_best={float(_POOL[0][0])}, t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.0f}s")

    return final_list
```
